# OpenGamepadUI tertiary hardware inventory

OpenGamepadUI **0.46.1**, research snapshot `0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c` (commit 2026-10-05, captured 2026-10-06). InputPlumber **0.81.0** `ea60d873cca17edd1cb655ede26f557108135252` (2026-09-14), confirmed equal to upstream HEAD on 2026-10-09. PowerStation **0.8.3** `b7f6def5b5e251fc19b15251723963aabf5f442d`, boundary-only upstream HEAD inspection on 2026-10-09.

**25 first-party platform presets + 1 packaging hardware row + 62 inherited handheld candidate profiles; 18 peripheral/generic/policy profiles excluded.** These are provenance rows, not distinct canonical devices; overlap is intentional. Final target remains HC/HHD union. Every dependency-only candidate, including ARM/Android-oriented profiles and newer x86 models, awaits reconciliation.

## Provenance and actual hardware boundary

Source priority is **HC > HHD > OpenGamepadUI**. Direct OGUI DMI detection selects UI resources; most resources only declare empty icon/action lists. It neither establishes input capabilities nor independently implements handheld input protocols. The detector comment explicitly asks to add recent AYANEO/GPD/OXP models. [core/global/platform.gd:66](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/core/global/platform.gd#L66)

Input device discovery, composite-device routing, profiles, virtual controller targets and interception are owned by the **InputPlumber** Linux service over `org.shadowblip.InputPlumber`. OGUI supplies configuration/UI and service proxies. Power/TDP/SMT controls are delegated to **PowerStation** over `org.shadowblip.PowerStation`. [extensions/core/src/input/inputplumber.rs:25](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/extensions/core/src/input/inputplumber.rs#L25); [extensions/core/src/performance/powerstation.rs:19](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/extensions/core/src/performance/powerstation.rs#L19)

OGUI sysext downloads the latest release of each service at packaging time, with no fixed compatible version or commit in these rules. Arch depends on InputPlumber and lists PowerStation as optional. The dependency commits in this inventory are research pins, not asserted shipped runtime versions. [Makefile:452](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/Makefile#L452); [Makefile:457](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/Makefile#L457); [package/archlinux/PKGBUILD:11](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/package/archlinux/PKGBUILD#L11); [package/archlinux/PKGBUILD:14](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/package/archlinux/PKGBUILD#L14)

PowerStation has Linux AMD providers including ASUS WMI, hwmon, RyzenAdj and ACPI power profiles, and Intel RAPL powercap/sysfs control. OGUI exposes CPU/GPU performance, boost/TDP and clock controls; a fan control provider/API was not found in the inspected boundary. Per-device fan support remains unknown. [src/performance/gpu/amd/tdp.rs:31](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/PowerStation/blob/b7f6def5b5e251fc19b15251723963aabf5f442d/src/performance/gpu/amd/tdp.rs#L31); [src/performance/gpu/intel/tdp.rs:37](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/PowerStation/blob/b7f6def5b5e251fc19b15251723963aabf5f442d/src/performance/gpu/intel/tdp.rs#L37); [src/performance/gpu/asus/asus_wmi.rs:66](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/PowerStation/blob/b7f6def5b5e251fc19b15251723963aabf5f442d/src/performance/gpu/asus/asus_wmi.rs#L66)

Windows-first .NET implementation requires Windows transports and policy. Linux DMI/sysfs, hidraw, evdev, IIO, udev, D-Bus and systemctl evidence is useful protocol/detection research, not a Windows implementation. No hardware or application runtime was touched.

Status meanings: **implemented** = concrete source path exists; **declared** = configuration/preset states it; **unsupported** = source explicitly blocks/omits that mechanism; **unknown** = evidence insufficient. None means hardware acceptance passed. InputPlumber DMI fields within one alternative are conjunctive; alternatives are disjunctive and values use glob matching. [src/config/mod.rs:1050](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/InputPlumber/blob/ea60d873cca17edd1cb655ede26f557108135252/src/config/mod.rs#L1050)

## Inherited InputPlumber handheld candidate profiles

All `50-*.yaml` handheld/convertible candidate rows below belong to the dependency provenance chain. A source marked blocked is retained for identity/duplication policy and must not be counted as working input. Full exact source selectors, mount matrices, target declarations and evidence are in the companion JSON.

### Anbernic Win 600

`opengamepadui:inputplumber:anbernic_win600`

Identity variants: Win600.

Matches: dmi.product_name=Win600 [rootfs/usr/share/inputplumber/devices/50-anbernic_win600.yaml:19](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/InputPlumber/blob/ea60d873cca17edd1cb655ede26f557108135252/rootfs/usr/share/inputplumber/devices/50-anbernic_win600.yaml#L19); dmi.sys_vendor=Anbernic [rootfs/usr/share/inputplumber/devices/50-anbernic_win600.yaml:20](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/InputPlumber/blob/ea60d873cca17edd1cb655ede26f557108135252/rootfs/usr/share/inputplumber/devices/50-anbernic_win600.yaml#L20).

## Concrete gaps and proof limits

- **No exact InputPlumber/PowerStation runtime pin in OpenGamepadUI distribution. (unknown)** Latest release artifacts are downloaded; inspected dependency HEAD snapshots are research pins, not proof of shipped versions. [Makefile:452](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/Makefile#L452); [Makefile:457](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/Makefile#L457)
- **First-party model detector lags dependency profiles and explicitly marks recent AYANEO/GPD/OXP TODO. (declared)** Inherited InputPlumber entries cover newer models without corresponding first-party OGUI preset. [core/global/platform.gd:66](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/core/global/platform.gd#L66)
- **No fan control provider/API found in inspected OGUI core/performance layer. (unknown)** PowerStation boundary is CPU/GPU/TDP/power-profile control. Repository tree and targeted power paths were checked; no hardware fan support is inferred from TDP support or external plugins. [extensions/core/src/performance/powerstation.rs:1](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/extensions/core/src/performance/powerstation.rs#L1); [core/systems/performance/power_station.gd:5](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/core/systems/performance/power_station.gd#L5); [src/performance/gpu/dbus/tdp.rs:32](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/PowerStation/blob/b7f6def5b5e251fc19b15251723963aabf5f442d/src/performance/gpu/dbus/tdp.rs#L32)
- **Windows transports and access contracts are absent. (unsupported)** Windows-first .NET library needs native device detection, HID/serial/IMU/OEM/power adapters. Linux service/UI implementation cannot be copied as Windows support. [core/systems/hardware/hardware_manager.gd:39](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/core/systems/hardware/hardware_manager.gd#L39); [extensions/core/src/input/inputplumber.rs:25](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/extensions/core/src/input/inputplumber.rs#L25); [rootfs/usr/share/opengamepadui/scripts/manage_input:23](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/OpenGamepadUI/blob/0db5812f21e98d0a5ac70bb1b5a674f2f369c22c/rootfs/usr/share/opengamepadui/scripts/manage_input#L23)
- **Hardware/firmware and per-variant feature acceptance unverified. (unknown)** This inventory is static source research. No daemon, controller, hardware write, build, or runtime action executed.
- **Profiles are authoritative declarations, not exhaustive per-model protocol decoding. (unknown)** All handheld candidate profile identities, transports, maps and target declarations are enumerated. Only selected useful driver output/motion facts inspected; external plugin implementations and every low-level input driver not audited. [src/config/mod.rs:1050](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/InputPlumber/blob/ea60d873cca17edd1cb655ede26f557108135252/src/config/mod.rs#L1050)

## Completeness

Enumerated every tracked OGUI handheld resource (25), direct model-dispatch alternatives, the AYANEO 3 module permission rule, every InputPlumber `50-*.yaml` handheld/convertible candidate profile (62), referenced capability-map labels and exact source/target event facts in JSON, and every other device-profile file (18) as excluded. JSON sourceFiles records 169 pinned source files. Selected Steam Deck, MSI Claw, Ally, Legion Go 2 and ONEXPLAYER X1 driver facts were inspected. This is a profile/fact inventory, not an exhaustive low-level driver audit or runtime/hardware support matrix.

## Useful dependency protocol details

- AYANEO Pocket DS has an output-only DirectInput rumble source for USB HID `4001:0428`: eight-byte output, left motor byte 4 and right motor byte 5 (zero-based). Upstream source records prior physical motor verification; no new hardware check was performed. Steam Deck haptic events are ignored by this source. [Driver facts:20,43](https://github.com/ShadowBlip/InputPlumber/blob/ea60d873cca17edd1cb655ede26f557108135252/src/input/source/hidraw/ayaneo_haptics.rs#L43).
- Companion JSON expands OEM capability-map labels into exact source keyboard/gamepad events and target events, preserving evidence per mapping. There are 70 profile-to-map bindings and 422 mapping occurrences (shared map reuse included).
